- **codex-review**: Run OpenAI Codex as a second, independent reviewer over a plan or a diff and surface what it finds. Advisory only: it never blocks, commits, or edits. Diff reviews go through the official openai/codex-plugin-cc companion (structured verdicts), plan reviews through the codex CLI. Output streams in bright green and is logged to .git/codex-review-&lt;branch&gt;.log. Use when the user types /codex-review, when a prompt carries --review, or when you want a cross-model second opinion. - **codex-review**: Cross-validate Claude Code's implementation plan or code changes using OpenAI Codex CLI as an independent second opinion.
